The observable proof that the strategy removes three leading characters rather than a comm prefix is the pairing of `@JsonProperty("mSortC")` [src/Z-OS-Connect-Payment-Interface/src/main/java/com/ibm/cics/cip/bank/springboot/paymentinterface/jsonclasses/paymentinterface/DbcrJson.java:L21] with `private int commSortC = 0;` [src/Z-OS-Connect-Payment-Interface/src/main/java/com/ibm/cics/cip/bank/springboot/paymentinterface/jsonclasses/paymentinterface/DbcrJson.java:L22], the Swagger property for that field being mSortC and not SortC | consume | src/Z-OS-Connect-Payment-Interface/src/main/java/com/ibm/cics/cip/bank/springboot/paymentinterface/jsonclasses/paymentinterface/DbcrJson.java | L21-L22 |
